>
> Retreating to MUN is the safest and most flexible option. It preserves my unit, keeps me near my home centers for potential builds, and I can redeploy from there. PIC is tempting for pressure but risky if France wants to push further. MAR is clearly off the table.
>
> I'll retreat A BUR to MUN to regroup and plan a new approach for Fall 1902.
